WM LinkedIn PRM · Creative dashboard

Last 60 days: Sep 11 – Nov 9, 2026 · ads with spend of $700 and above · same-name ads running in several ad sets are combined into one (sum ÷ sum), with their ad sets listed · TLA posts in their own tab (same post across ad sets combined) · in-platform LinkedIn (Funnel Gate), never BI · Hard SU = rule 18155186, QE = rule 18155194 · Airtable excluded · LAN ads are only benchmarked against their own LAN ad set · totals are sum ÷ sum · refreshed every Monday · pick another date range at the top
How verdicts work (recommend-only, nothing is changed automatically): Verdicts are on the combined ad, or on just the selected country / ad set when the top filter is used. Its benchmark = what its ad sets' own CPHSU would predict for that spend (weighted). 🏆 Winner = live somewhere, ≥5 Hard SU, CPHSU at least 15% below that benchmark and ≤ $450. ⏸️ Pause = ≤1 Hard SU on ≥ $900, or CPHSU over 30% above the benchmark and over $360. ⚠️ = a weak copy of an otherwise OK ad in one ad set. Ad-level SU counts are small; read them as signals. TLA posts are judged the same way, against their own TLA ad set (deep TLA-vs-regular analysis stays on the TLA board). Any pause needs approval in the group.
📅 Dates   🔎 Country Ad set Numbers on cards, tables and totals are recalculated for the selection (sum ÷ sum). Verdicts (Winner / Pause / Watch) are re-decided for the selection too.
